   tamiḻp pāppuṉaintu īcaṉait tutittut tamakkup poṉ vēṇṭumeṉap
   pirārttittār. īcaṉum avarukku aruḷpuriyak karuti tamatu pūtakaṇaṅkaḷai
   ēvi, ammalaippāṟaiyil poṉṉaik koṇarntu coriyak kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭār. avvāṟē
   coriyappaṭṭa poṉṉai nāyaṉār eṭuttukkoṇṭu patikam oṉṟu pāṭit tutittār.
   īcaṉum aṅkuḷḷa oṉṟu pāti eṉṉum iṭattil cuntararkkuk kāṭci aḷittār.
   cuntararum aṅkiruntē malaināṭu ēkiṉār. (cuntarar pāṭiya ippatikam
   ippōtu kiṭaikka villai .)
   paṉṉiraṇṭām paṅku peṟṟatu
   tañcaiyai āṇṭu vanta cōḻamaṉṉaṉ oruvaṉ kāvirip paṭṭiṉattil vāḻntu vanta
   ceṭṭikulattaic cērnta peṇṇai maṇakka niṉaittāṉ. itai aṉumatikkamuṭiyāta
   nilaiyil kāvirippaṭṭiṉamē tīkku iraiyākkappaṭṭatu. anta ūriliruntu
   tappi vantavarkaḷ patiṉoru ceṭṭi kulattiṉaic cērntavarkaḷ.
   avarkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭaṉ eṭuttuvanta poruṭcelvaṅkaḷai irattiṉakiri malaiyil
   vaittuttāṉ paṅku vaittārkaḷ. āḷukkoru paṅkāka patiṉoru paṅkākap
   pirittaṉar. āṉāl celvam paṉṉiraṇṭu paṅkukaḷākat tāṉākavē pirintatu.
   ettuṇai muṟai pākam pirittapōtilum attuṇai muṟaikaḷilum mīṇṭum mīṇṭum
   paṉṉiraṇṭu paṅkukaḷākavē pirintatu. itaṉāl viyappuṟṟu niṉṟa avarkaḷ muṉ
   iṟaivaṉ tōṉṟi, “eṉ iṭattil vaittuc celvaṅkaḷaip pirikkum nīṅkaḷ eṉakkum
   oru paṅku taravēṇṭum,” eṉṟu kēṭṭuk koṇṭār. atu mutal avarkaḷukkup
   ‘paṉṉiraṇṭām ceṭṭiyārkaḷ' eṉṟa peyar vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu.
   inta vakuppiṉar pala ūrkaḷil paravi irukkiṉṟaṉar. ic camūkattiṉar
   mēṟkūṟiya irattiṉakirīcuvarar malaiyil iṉṟaḷavum kālaiccanti
   kaṭṭaḷaiyiṉaic ciṟappuṟa naṭatti varukiṉṟaṉar. aḻakiya nantavaṉam oṉṟai
   amaittup puṣpattoṇṭu ceytum, pāl apiṣēkattukkākap pacumaṭam oṉṟu
   niṟuvip pāl vaḻaṅkiyum iṟaivarkkut tāṅkaḷ paṅkāḷar eṉpatai iṉṟum
   kāṭṭivarukiṟārkaḷ. 'koṇṭāṭum paṉṉiruvar kūṭṭam paṇi ceyya eṉṟum, (198)
   'paṉṉiruvar pālukanta paṉṉiruvar pōṟṟiyatu paṉṉiruvar vāḻttavē,
   paṉṉiruvar eṉṉuṭaṉē eṉṟu varunāṭaṉ' (393) eṉṟa aṭikaḷil paṉṉiraṇṭām
   ceṭṭiyārkaḷiṉ teyvappaṇikaḷai ulāvāciriyar pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār. akkāla mutal
   iṉṟum avarkaḷ īṭṭiya poruḷil paṉṉiraṇṭil oru pākattai iṟaivaṉukkuk
   kāṇikkaiyākki varukiṟārkaḷ. taṅkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷiṭam paṅkupeṟṟa avviṟaivar
   ataṟku naṉṟiyāka, viḻākkālaṅkaḷil tām tēr mītamarntu pavaṉivaruvatupōla
   avarkaḷum taṅkaḷ kaliyāṇa kālaṅkaḷil tērmītēṟi pavaṉivarum ciṟappai
   nalkiyuḷḷaṉar. ammāṇikkaveṟpaṉ taṅkaḷukkaruḷiya pēraruḷiṉ
   ñāpakārttamāka apperumāṉuṭaiya tēraip putiya muṟaiyil muṉṉaiyōrkaḷ
   ceytamaittārkaḷ. antat tēriltāṉ irattiṉakiri āṇṭavar aṉṟum
   pavaṉivantār, iṉṟum pavaṉi varukiṟār - intac ceyalkaḷ yāvum
   ulāvāciriyarāl aḻakuṟap pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.*
   “kūṭṭupati ṉoṉṟāṉa kōnakarattār muṉpōy
       nāṭṭumitil nāmōr nakarameṉa- tēṭṭamatāy
       īcaṉār colla icaivukoṇṭa nāḷmutalāy
       mācilā (tu) ōṅkum makimaiyuṭaṉ- tēcamēl
       paṉṉiru varākip pala ticaiyum kīrtticela
       maṉṉiyē vāḻum vaṇikar aṉpāy- piṉṉiyaṟṟum
       cempoṉ maṇittērēṟit tēviyuṭaṉ''
       pavaṉi varukiṉṟār eṉpatu anta ulāk kaṇṇikaḷ (119-123).

   (* paṉṉiraṇṭām ceṭṭimāraip paṟṟiya varalāṟṟai Appendix II pakkam 1-2l
   pārkka).
   kalveṭṭukaḷ
   ittalattaip paṟṟiya kalveṭṭukaḷil irattiṉakiriyil 50 kalveṭṭukaḷum,
   civāyam kōyilil 7 kalveṭṭukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. ivai teṉṉintiyak kalveṭṭut
   tokutikaḷil 1914 ām āṇṭil 144 mutal 191 eṇkaḷākavum; 1892 il 102, 103
   eṇkaḷākavum; civāyam kōyilil uḷḷavai 1913 ām āṇṭil 44 mutal 50 varaiyil
   uḷḷa eṇkaḷākavum pativu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   ivai cuvāmi ammaṉ kōyilkaḷil uḷḷa cuṟṟuccuvarkaḷilum, malaiyiṉ
   pāṟaikaḷilum veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ikkalveṭṭukaḷāl paḻamaiyāṉa pala
   ceytikaḷ teriya varukiṉṟaṉa.
   kalveṭṭuc ceytikaḷiṉ curukkam
   immalai, māṇikkamalai, vāṭpōkki eṉṟum cuvāmikkuk kuṉṟam uṭaiyār,
   tirumāṇikkamalai uṭaiya tampirāṉār, makātēvar, nāyaṉār, vāṭpōkki
   makātēvar eṉṟum; ammaṉukku haravaṭṭēsvari (haravakṣēsvari) eṉṟum;
   ivvūrp pakuti. teṉkarai rājakampīra vaḷanāṭṭaic cērnta kuṟunakar nāṭu
   eṉṟum peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅki vantiruppaṉavākat teriya varukiṟatu.
   ki. pi. 1783-l malaimēl uḷḷa kōyil maṇṭapaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭatum, 13-vatu
   nūṟṟāṇṭil hoycaḷa maṉṉaṉākiya vīracōmēsvaraṉatu āṭciyil innāṭṭiṉ
   perumpakuti iruntu vantatum pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa. ivaṉ peyarāl cōmēsvaraṉ
   pēṭṭai (cōmacarampēṭṭai) eṉṟa ūrum iruppatai aṟiyalām. atu ippōtu
   cōmaracam pēṭṭai eṉa vaḻaṅki varukiṟatu.
   cuvāmi cannitikkup pōkum mūṉṟu varicaippaṭikkaṭṭukaḷ rāciṅkatēvaṉ
   eṉpavaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. civapātacēkarapuram eṉṉum
   pakutiyil iruntavarkaḷ, ikkōyilukkup pala tāṉaṅkaḷaic ceytu uḷḷārkaḷ.
   rājarāja cōḻatēvaṉ, jaṭāvarmaṉ, cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ, vijayanakara
   maṉṉarkaḷāṉa catāciva makārāyar, vīranaracimma pujapala makārāyar,
   viruppaṇṇa uṭaiyār, kōṉērimēk koṇṭāṉ (kōṉ - nēr - iṉmai koṇṭāṉ,)
   kulōttuṅka cōḻarkaḷ, vikkirama cōḻaṉ, hoycaḷa maṉṉaṉ vīrarāmanātatēvaṉ
   mutalāṉa aracarkaḷiṉ āṭcik kālaṅkaḷil kōyil pūjaikkum,
   viḷakkiṭuvataṟkum, kāviriyiliruntu kuṭaṅkaḷil nīrkoṇṭu varuvataṟkum,
   tiruviḻākkaḷ naṭattuvataṟkum, veṟṟilai pākku naivēttiyattiṟkum
   nilaṅkaḷum poṟkācukaḷum paṇamum, tulaiyuṟai cempoṉṉum taṉittaṉiyē tāṉam
   ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   161 - vatu eṇ kalveṭṭuppaṭi, ciṟṟamparuṭaiyāṉ eṉpavaṉāl takṣiṇāmūrtti
   cilaiyum, tiruppaṭikaḷum amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vevvēṟu aracarkaḷiṉ
   kālaṅkaḷil vevvēṟu tiruppaṇikaḷ naṭaipeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avvappōtu kōyilukku
   ēṟpaṭṭa tāṉatarumaṅkaḷ, aṟacceyalkaḷ, ākiyavaikaḷaip paṟṟic cila
   kalveṭṭukaḷ terivikkiṉṟaṉa.
   pērvari, cilvari, veṭṭivētiṉai, kulaitāmpu, ceynīr veṭṭi, mutaliya
   peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kācu, āṉaiaccu, tulaiuṟai cempoṉ mutaliya
   peyarkaḷ akkālattu nāṇayaṅkaḷait terivikkiṉṟaṉa. karuṅkīḻppaḷḷi,
   etirili cōḻa nallūr, vīracōḻa caturvēti maṅkalam, civapātacēkarapuram.
   vallaṭa maṅkalam, cātta maṅkalam, cōḻapāṇṭipuram mutaliya ūrppeyarkaḷ
   collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   ittalattaip paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ
   arataṉamalai, kākam paṟavāmalai, vāṭpōkkimalai, civataipuri,
   pañcākkaramalai, cokkarmalai eṉap pala tirunāmaṅkaḷāl aḻaikkappaṭum.
   immalaivāḻ iṟaivaṉ iṟaiviyarait tirunāvukkaracar tām pāṭiya tēvārattil,
   'tiruvāṭpōkki tirukkuṟuntokai' yil pattu pākkaḷāl pāṭiyirukkiṟār. avar
   pāṭiya tiruttāṇṭakamum uḷḷatu. aruṇakirinātar pāṭiya tiruppukaḻil
   ittalam paṟṟiya irupāṭalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. umāpati civāccāriyār iyaṟṟiya
   civakṣēttira civanāmakkali veṇpā eṉṉum nūlil ittalattaik
   kuṟippiṭukiṟār. tiricirapuram makāvittuvāṉ mīṉāṭcicuntaram
   piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ ittalam paṟṟi oru kalampakam iyaṟṟiyuḷḷār. ataṟku
   vāṭpōkkik kalampakam eṉṟu peyar. 1910, 1925 - l ṭākṭar aiyaravarkaḷ
   veḷiyiṭṭa makāvittuvāṉ mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ pirapantat
   tiraṭṭiṉ pakutiyil mūlamum, 1976-il u. vē. cāmināta aiyar nūl
   nilaiyattārāl taṉiyē kuṟippuraiyuṭaṉum veḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu. 'civastala
   mañcari' eṉṉum nūlil ittalam paṟṟiyum kāviri tīrttam talapurāṇa
   varalāṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ‘irattiṉacala māṉmiyam' eṉṉum nūl yāḻppāṇam
   tāmōtaram piḷḷaiyāl urainaṭaiyil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. tiruvārūr vaittiyanāta
   tēcikar mutaliyōrāl ceyyuḷil ceyyappeṟṟa ‘tiruvāṭpōkki purāṇam'
   oṉṟuḷḷatu. itu naimicacarukkam mutal poṉṉiṭum pāṟaic carukkam varai 14
   carukkaṅkaḷaik koṇṭatu. 1911 - ām āṇṭil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. purāṇak
   kataikaḷai eḷitil terintu koḷḷum poruṭṭu alakarai vākavi tiru. a.
   cuppiramaṇiya pāratiyārāl vacaṉarūpamāka eḻutappaṭṭa vacaṉa
   curukkaṅkaḷum ittuṭaṉ cērkkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa. māṇikkamalai nātar pēril
   ‘tiruttoṇṭar catakam' tiruppāṇṭik koṭumuṭi malaikkoḻuntu kavirāyar
   pāṭiyatu, accāṉatu. veṇpāppuli ti. cu. vēlucāmippiḷḷaiyavarkaḷ ‘tēvāra
   civatalaveṇpā' eṉṉum nūlil,
   'muṉpāṉ mulaiccuvaṭu mōlimēl vāṭṭaḻumpu
       piṉpāṟ pirampuvaṭup peṟṟavaṉai - aṉpārnta
       vāṭpōkki yāṉainaṉku vāḻttāma lēvāḷā
       nāṭpōkki yāṉaikiṉ ṟaṉaṉ.'

   eṉṟa aḻakiya pāṭalait tantuḷḷār. tiru. vē. muttucāmi aiyarum kavirāca
   paṇṭita kaṉakarāja aiyarum cērntu pāṭiya 'vāṭpōkkiīcar iraṭṭai
   maṇimālai' eṉṉum nūlum uḷḷatu (1934). civāyamalaik kōyil varalāṟu eṉṉum
   nūl R. pañcanatam piḷḷaiyavarkaḷāl 1950 - ām āṇṭil veḷiyiṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
   1968 - l vāṭpōkki veṇpā antāti eṉṉum nūlum tiruvācakamaṇi K. M.
   pālacuppiramaṇiyaṉ avarkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu.

                   vāṭpōkki ulāviṉ poruṭ curukkam

   ivvulāvil mutal oṉpatu kaṇṇikaḷil civaperumāṉ perumai kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
   avaṉ teyvaṅkaḷukkellām mēlāṉavaṉ (teyvapparāparaṉ); avaṉē kāṇpāṉum
   kāṭciyumāy viḷaṅkukiṟāṉ; āṉantamum aṟivum avaṉē; avaṉ antamillāt
   taṉimutal; kālattiṉ ellai kaṭantavaṉ; ōṅkāramāki niṉṟavaṉ; uruvamum
   aruvamumāki niṉṟavaṉ.
   10-āvatu mutal 32ām kaṇṇivarai, vāṭpōkkit talacciṟappu kūṟappeṟukiṟatu.
   ittalattai aṇukip pēṟupeṟṟōr varalāṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ittalattu īcaṉ
   cūriyamaṇṭalam otta oḷivīcum māṇikkaveṟpai iṭamākak koṇṭavaṉ. avaṉ taṉ
   peyaraiyum vāṭpōkki eṉṟē amaittuk koṇṭāṉ. urōmaca muṉivarukkum
   camaravēl eṉṉum aracaṉukkum aruḷiyavaṉ; ceṅkatirōṉ acurarkaḷ ceykiṉṟa
   tīmaiyiṉiṉṟu viṭupaṭa ittalattaiyaṭaintu taṉ iṭar nīṅkap peṟṟāṉ;
   iṭikkaṭavuḷ tutikka avaṉukku varamaḷittāṉ; intiraṉ, akattiyar, āriya
   aracaṉ ivarkaḷukku aruḷiyavaṉ. turkkaiyiṉ pirammakatti tōṣattai
   nīkkiyavaṉ ; toṇṭarkaḷ tavamceyya pañcākkarakkukaiyai niruviyavaṉ.
   nēriyarkōṉ peṇṭirukkup poṉṉāram kāṭṭiyavaṉ; vāyutēvaṉ pūcikka avaṉukku
   varam koṭuttōṉ; murukēcaṉ eṉum paktaṉaḷittat tūṇṭāviḷakkai
   ēṟṟaruḷiyavaṉ eṉṟu palavitamāka ittalattu īcaṉiṉ perumai kūṟap
   paṭukiṟatu.
   33-vatu mutal 45-ām kaṇṇivarai tīrttac ciṟappu collappaṭukiṟatu.
   avaikaḷ varumāṟu: catākati, tīrttam, kulica tīrttam, nāka tīrttam,
   cūriya tīrttam, kaja tīrttam, urōmaca tīrttam, turkkā tīrttam,
   parattuvāca tīrttam eṉpavai eṭṭuttikkukaḷilum uḷḷaṉa. ivaitavira,
   pañcākkaraccuṉai, teyvaccuṉai, varākaccuṉai ivaikaḷum iṅku uḷḷaṉa.
   mēlum, ittalattu īcaṉ vēppamarattait talavirukṣamākak koṇṭavaṉ;
   nīlakaṇṭa cōḻaṉ āṟṟiya paṇiviṭaiyai ēṟṟu avaṉukku naṟkatiyaruḷiyavaṉ;
   muṉivar eṇmarukku varam īntavaṉ; ciṟṟāyaṉārkkut taṉṉaiyē tiyākam
   ceytavaṉ; yāvar aṇukiṉum varaiyātu varamaḷippavaṉ.
   46-āvatu kaṇṇimutal 57- ām kaṇṇivarai, kākam aṇukāk kaṉakiriyāṉ, kāvēri
   āṟṟiṉāṉ: kuṉṟucūḻ nāṭṭiṉāṉ; civāya nakariṉāṉ; yōkak kutiraiyāṉ;
   meyññāṉa āṉaiyāṉ; poṉṉitaḻimālaiyāṉ; nātamuraciṉāṉ; ēṟṟuk koṭiyiṉāṉ;
   añceḻuttiṉ āṇaiyāṉ eṉa iṟaivaṉukkuriya pattu aṅkaṅkaḷum pēcap
   peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
   58-ām kaṇṇi mutal 71-ām kaṇṇivarai irattiṉakiri īcaṉiṉ perum pukaḻ
   pēcappaṭukiṉṟatu. iṅku, mutal nāṉku kaṇṇikaḷum maṭakku eṉṉum aṇiyil
   amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. piṉṉar, pañcāṅkaṅkaḷil naṭcattiram oḻinta titi vāra
   yōka karaṇam eṉṉum nāṉkum kūṟappeṟukiṉṟaṉa; cittirai mutal paṅkuṉi
   varai paṉṉiraṇṭu mātaṅkaḷiṉ peyaruṭaṉ iṇaittu iṟaivaṉ pukaḻ
   pēcappaṭukiṟatu. cittirai mātattil tērōṭṭut tirunāḷil īcaṉ pavaṉi
   varukiṟāṉ.
   72-āvatu mutal 74-ām kaṇṇivarai cūriya maṇṭalam pōl pirakācikkum
   maṇṭapattil vīṟṟiruntu kāppaṇintu, koṭiyēṟṟi vītikulavum ceyti
   collappaṭukiṟatu.
   75-āvatu kaṇṇimutal 117-ām kaṇṇivarai kēcātipātamāka iṟaivaṉ aṇinta
   aṇikaḷiṉ vakai kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. neṟṟiyil tiruccāntu tilakam, mārpil
   veṇpaṭṭu, koṉṟaimālai, cantaṉakkuḻampu, muṉivarkaḷ veṇcāmarai vīcutal;
   peṇkaḷ pala kītam pāṭal; teṉṟal ulavutal; nilavu eṟittal; mātavarkaḷ
   cūḻntu pukaḻntuvaral - ivvita alaṅkāra vicēṣaṅkaḷuṭaṉ īcaṉ paḷḷiyaṟai
   cērtalum tiruppaḷḷi eḻucciyum 75 mutal 90 varaiyuḷḷa kaṇṇikaḷil
   kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   piṉṉar, poṉṉinīr āṭiya tiruccaṭai, cempoṉ āṭai, āticēṭaṉ utarapantaṉam,
   mūmmūrttikaḷum oruṅkiṇaintāṟ pōṉṟa muppuri nūl, maṇimakuṭam,
   nutaṟpaṭṭam, kātiṟ poṟkuḻaiyum tōṭum, mārpil poṉmālai
   vēymuttukkaṇṭacaram, tōḷil kēyūram, tōḷ vaḷaiyam, maṇippatakkam,
   maṇimālai, kaikaḷil maḻu, māṉ, tuṭi, ivaikaḷ varṇikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa
   (91-108)
   iṟaivaṉ civikaiyēṟiyapōtu talaimālai āṭiyatu. caṭaiyum piṟaiyum
   alaimōti āṭiṉa, koṉṟaimālai tuvaṇṭāṭiyatu, caṅkam atirntatu, taṭāri,
   muḻavu, muracuttokai ivai atirntaṉa. īcaṉ tiruvāṭu taṇṭēṟi varukaiyil
   cilar veṇkuṭai niḻaṟṟavum, cilar veṇcāmarai acaikkavum, cilar ālavaṭṭam
   vīcavum, cilar kāḷāñci ēnti varavum cilar cempoṟpirampu ēnti varavum,
   ākiya nikaḻccikaḷ collappaṭukiṉ ṟaṉa.(109-117)
   ivvāṟu civikaiyēṟi, pirakārattil ulāvanta īcaṉ kōpuravācal aṭaintu
   poṉṉiṭu pāṟaikkarukil paṉṉireṇṭām ceṭṭimār amaittuttanta, cempoṉ
   maṇittēr ēṟutalai 118-123 kaṇṇikaḷ kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
   tērēṟi īcaṉ amarntavuṭaṉ taṭāri, tuṭi, timilai, taṇṇumai, kaittāḷam,
   muracu, maṇi, mēḷam, kuṭamuḻā, caṅku, timiri, tavil, nākacuram, maṅkala
   kītam, vāttiyaṅkaḷ muḻaṅkiṉamaiyai 124, 125- ām kaṇṇikaḷ varṇikkiṉṟaṉa.
   126, 127-ām kaṇṇikaḷil piccam, tuvacam, cantiravaṭṭai mutalāṉa
   ciṉṉaṅkaḷ cūḻntu varutal kuṟikkappaṭukiṟatu.
   irattiṉakiri īcar pavaṉi varutalaikkāṇa piramaṉ mutaliyōr tamtam
   vākaṉaṅkaḷil varutalai 128-137 kaṇṇikaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. piramaṉ
   aṉṉattiṉmēlum, tirumāl karuṭaṉmēlum, vīrapākutēvaṉ iṭapattiṉmēlum,
   tēvarkaḷ vimāṉat tērkaḷilum, vināyakaṉ peruccāḷi vākaṉattilum, murukaṉ
   mayil mītum, eṇticaik kāvalarkaḷ mā, maṟi, kaṭā, pūtam, mīṉ, māṉ,

   ūrtiyilum, cāttaṉ yāṉai mēlum vantu kuḻumukiṉṟaṉar. capta kaṉṉimār,

   vimāṉattil ēṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. vityātararum kantarvarum vantu cērntu
   koḷkiṉṟaṉar. ñāṉacampantar, tirunāvukkaracar, cuntarar, māṇikkavācakar,
   caṇṭēcar ākiyōrum civikaiyil ūrntu vantu cērukiṉṟaṉar.

   172-āvatu mutal 204-ām kaṇṇivarai pētaiyiṉ avayava varuṇaṉaiyum, piṉṉar

   tuṇaimulaiyāḷ eṉṟellām varuṇikkiṟār.
   pētaiyiṉ ceyalkaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟuṅkāl aṇipuṉaiya eṉakku vaṇṇa mulaiyaik

   vāveṉṟaḻaippāḷ; eṉ arukil ivar vārārō eṉpāḷ. appōtu aṉṉaiyar avaḷaip
   pārttu īcaṉār amararkku aṉṟu amutattaiyē paṭaittaḷittavaṉ; iṉṟu uṉ
   maṇaṟcōṟṟai virumpi varuvāṉō; accōṟu avaṉēṟum viṭaikkē (tirumāl)
   uriyatu eṉpār; civaperumāṉpāl ettakaiya aṉpuṇarcciyum illātiruntum

   pētaiyiṉ taṉmaiyai naṉku viḷakkukiṟār āciriyar.
   205- ām kaṇṇi mutal petumpaiyiṉ avayava varuṇaṉaiyum avaḷ nilā
   muṟṟattil tōḻiyarkaḷcūḻak kaḻaṅkāṭutalum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   aṟintum aṟiyāpparuvattiṉaḷ ātaliṉ pakkuvamāyp paṉṉum mukaiyaṉṉa

   ēykkum cāyalāḷ, kuyilaippaḻikkum maḻalaiyāḷ eṉṟum varṇikkiṟār.
   219-ām kaṇṇimutal 228 varai petumpai kaḻalāṭal kūṟappeṟukiṟatu. 229

   kaṇṇi 252 mutal 280 varai maṅkaipparuvattāḷiṉ uṭaluṟuppiṉ varuṇaṉaiyum

   vītikku eḻuntaruḷiṉamai kūṟappeṟukiṟatu.
   maṭantaiyiṉ uṭalamaippum ceyalkaḷum 281-ām kaṇṇi mutal 314-ām
   kaṇṇivarai kūṟappaṭum.
   piṉṉi muṭitta avaḷ kūntalum atil aṇintirunta piṟai eṉṉum aṇiyum,
   mēkattaiyum ataṉ naṭuvil oḷiviṭum miṉṉalaiyum ottiruppataiyum, avaḷ taṉ

   varāl pōṉṟa kālum, cilampolikkum pātamum varṇikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   inta nalaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya maṅkai yāḻaimīṭṭa etirākap pāṅkiyar paṇ
   pāṭutalai 296-297 kaṇṇikaḷil kūṟukiṟār.
   viṇṇilum, maṇṇilum, piṟa ulakaṅkaḷilum kāṇpataṟku aḻakiya talaivarai
   āyntu kūṟuṅkaḷ eṉa maṭantai tōḻiyarkaḷai viṉava, piruntai eṉum
   peṇṇaiyaṇaivataṟkākac cuṭukāṭṭil avaḷ iṟanta uṭalaip pulliya mālum, taṉ
   maṉaiviyāṉa carasvati takkayāttil mūkkiḻantum avaḷai nāvil vaittuḷḷa
   pirama ṉum, kōtamamuṉivaṉ cāpattāl uṭal muḻuvatum paṅkamuṟṟa intiraṉum,
   taṉ maṉaiviyai oru kuḷikaiyākat tiraṭṭik kaiyil vaittuḷḷa tēvaṉum,

   kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   kaṇṇikaḷ 303 mutal 306 varai tērmēl varupavaṉ peṇṇukkum āṇukkum
   pēraḻakaṉ, kaṇṇukkiṉiya vāṭpōkkiyāṉ, namaṉai mārkkaṇṭaṉukkāka ākattilē
   utaittavaṉ, takkayākattil tēvarkaḷaic citaittavaṉ, māṇikka
   malaikkoḻuntāka amarntu nākattiṉ pūcaiyai ēṟṟavaṉ, kāviriyiṉ nīrāl
   āṭṭappeṟum caṭaiyaiyuṭaiyavaṉ, eṉṟu pāṅkiyar kūṟṟu pēcappaṭukiṟatu.
   appaṭi aṇuki vanta iṟaivaṉai maṭantai pātamutal muṭivarai uṟṟunōkkit
   toḻukiṟāḷ. nīti vaḻuvāta nīr eṅkaḷ niṟaiyaik kavarvatu takumō; āṟṟait
   talaiyil uṭaiya nīr eṅkaḷukku āṟutalaic ceyyīrō; muṉṉar maṉmataṉai
   aṭakkak kaṇpaṭaitta nīr, eṅkaḷ kāmattai aṭakkak kaṇpaṭaikkātatu eṉṉa
   eṉṟu pēcum aḷavil, iṟaivaṉatu poṟṟēr teruviṉiṉṟum akaṉṟatu. ippaṭi
   viṉāviṭaiyāka amaintuḷḷa pakuti ivvulāviṉ ciṟappu amcamākum.
   315 mutal 343-ām kaṇṇi varai arivaiyiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟappaṭum. ivaḷ
   aṅkavaṉappu mutal 12 kaṇṇikaḷil varṇikkappaṭukiṟatu.
   mātarār aḻakellām tiraṭṭi alaṅkārattūrikaiyāl piramaṉ vaṭitta uruvamō,
   avaḷ kuḻal yukāntakālattiṟṟōṉṟum iruṭpiḻampō , mēruvaiyē taṉuvāka
   vaḷaittiṭṭa puruvamō eṉṟu tikaikkiṟār āciriyar. oḷirkaṇṇum,
   ciṟunakaiyum, vaṉamulaiyum, tuṭi iṭaiyum, aravu nāṇum alkulum,
   aravintamnēr pātamum uṭaiyavar itterivai. māmātarōṭu kūṭiyirunta ivaḷ
   māṇikkak kūttaṉai maiyaliṭaippaṭuttum upāyam yātu eṉa avarkaḷaik kēṭka,
   avarkaḷum uṉ kaṇṇīraik kaṇṭu avar kaṉivār; antap pēraḻakaṉ muṉ nī uṉ
   muḻu aḻakaiyum kāṭṭiṉāl kāmittu aṇaivar eṉkiṟārkaḷ. nāṇam mikunta
   terivaiyum cāmpacivaṉ kaṇpaṭṭāl pōtum avaṉai vacamākkik koḷvēṉ eṉṟu
   kūṟip peṇpaṭṭaṅ kaṭṭi mataṉ pōrukkup puṟappaṭukiṟāḷ. kūntal piṉṉaṇiyāyp
   piṟaḻa, alkul tērai muṉṉaṇiyatākki, mulaiyāṉaiyai muṉṉē celutti, tāṉai
   (cēṉai, cēlai)yoṭu naṭantu tākkukiṟāḷ. ippaṭit tākkuṅkāl pāṅkiyar
   avaḷai aṇuki maṉmataṉaiyē kaṇṇāl erittavar uṉṉaik kaṇṭu malaippārō;
   ceṅkāṭṭaṅkuṭiyil makavaik koṉṟavar uṉṉai ēṟparō; carapa pakṣiyāka
   urukkoṇṭu naraciṅkattaiyē kīṇṭavar uṉṉaikkaṇṭu tikaippārō; puliyiṉ
   aṅkattaiyē urittavar añcuvarō; poṅki eḻuntu etirvanta yāṉaiyaiyē
   kiḻittu ataṉ tōlai āṭaiyāka aṇintu koṇṭavar uṉ paṭaiyaik kaṇṭu
   mayaṅkuvarō? māṉē uṉ aṇivakuppāl āvatu eṉṉa, vā eṉṟu avarkaḷ
   tēṟṟumaḷavil, irattiṉakiri īcaṉiṉ tēr maṟaintu ceṉṟatu. katiravaṉ
   maṟaintu mālaivantu ivaḷai varuttat toṭaṅkiyatu. iruḷ eṉṟa nīlaneruppum
   nilāccoriyum veṇṇeruppum ivaḷai aṇuka piramaipiṭittavaḷ pōl niṉṟāḷ.
   344-ām kaṇṇi mutal 367-ām kaṇṇivarai terivaiyiṉ uṭaṟpolivu, avaḷ
   ceyalkaḷ ivai kūṟappaṭum.
   muṉ pāṟkaṭal kaṭaintapōtu ilakkumi uṇṭāṉāḷ; iṉṟu alaṅkāram eṉṉum
   kaṭalil, meynnalam eṉum mantarattai nāṭṭi, pēraḻaku eṉṟa kayiṟṟiṉāl
   kaṭaiya peṇṇamutāy vantutittavaḷ ivaḷ; kāmaracam eṉṉaveṉpatai
   naṉkaṟintavaḷ; vēl viḻiyāḷ; oyil naṭaiyāḷ; karuṅkuḻalāḷ ; kaccu aṟavē
   miṉṉum taṉattāḷ; miṉṉal pōṉṟa ciṟutta iṭaiyāḷ; āṭal makaḷirāṉa
   tilōttamaiyum mayaṅkiṭumpaṭiyāka naṭaṉa naṭaipōṭum pātattāḷ; mōkiṉitāṉ
   maṟumuṟai vantāḷō eṉap polivu peṟṟavaḷ. terivai uṭal muḻutum
   aṇikalaṉkaḷaip pūṇṭu taṉṉai naṉku alaṅkarittuk koṇṭu civaṉāriṉ
   kīrttiyaip pukal eṉṟu tāṉ vaḷarttuvanta kiḷḷaiyaik kēṭka atuvum īcaṉār
   perumaiyai eṭuttu uraikka, malaraṇai mēl amarntu oru mukūrtta nēram oru
   yukam āṉatupōla kēṭṭiruntāḷ. naṉku kuraleṭuttup pāvalarpōl pāṭiṉāḷ.
   vāṭpōkki īcaṉ eṉ etir varuvār eṉṟu makarayāḻaik kaikkoṇṭu canta
   icaipāṭum camayattil ampalattilē naṭaṉam āṭi āṭi pātattaḻumpum,
   vētanūlkaḷai ōti vāyttaḻumpum, vaikaināṭaṉ kaippirampāl aṭitta
   puṟattaḻumpum, yamaṉaik kālāl utaittaṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa kāltaḻumpum, kampā
   natikkaraiyil umaitaḻuva ataṉāl mārpilē patinta mulaittaḻumpum,
   mutukilē patinta vaḷaittaḻumpum, tēvarkaḷ tiṉam vaṇaṅkutalāl avarkaḷ
   muṭitākkiya aṭittaḻumpum, irattiṉakiriyil āriya aracaṉ, iṭi, nākam
   ivarkaḷāl ēṟpaṭṭa taḻumpum ēṟṟirukkum perumāṉ cittiramaṇittērēṟit
   teruvaṇuka ivaḷum taṉ vīṇaiyaip puṟattē eṟintāḷ. pāṇapattirar
   naḷḷiravil kūṭaṟ perumāṉmuṉ, elumpiṉai urukkum amuta icaiyiṉaip
   poḻintataik kēṭṭu navamaṇi iḻaitta poṟpalakai iṭṭa civaperumāṉmuṉ
   ceṉṟu, tēvārappāṭalkaḷaic cevimaṭuttu cuntararukkup poṉṉiṭṭa nīr, eṉ
   mārpil (tēmalai) poṉṉai aḻaikkap piṉṉiṭuvatu ēṉ; nakkīraṉār vēṇṭa
   carīrattaiyē utavip perumtiyāki eṉappēr koṇṭa nīr, eṉ pōṉṟa mātar moḻi
   kēḷāvakai ētu eṉṟellām iṟaiñca, maṉmataṉum ivaḷ virakakkaṉalai mēlum
   tūṇṭiviṭa, māmaṇit tēr teruvakaṉṟatu. terivaiyum aḻakiya māṭamoṉṟil
   pukuntiṭṭāḷ.
   368 mutal 408-ām kaṇṇi varai pēriḷampeṇṇiṉ uṭaṟpāṅkum kuṇa nalaṉkaḷum
   kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   tēvamātu pōluḷḷa ivaḷ avarkaḷukku muṉ utittavaḷ eṉalām. pākupōl iṉiya
   moḻiyāḷ, vacīkaram uṭaiyavaḷ, aṅkamellām pūraṇamāka mutirntu aḻaku
   tēṅkum meyyiṉāḷ, kalviyum ataṟkēṟpa oḻukkamum kūṭip paṇpaṭṭa nilaiyāḷ,
   malaiyam pōṉṟu taṭaiyiṉṟi vaḷarntatāl ippōtu carintu viḻuntatō, iṭai
   ciṟutta ciṅkattai nōkki vaṇaṅkutaṟkē matayāṉai cintittatō, mēlaināḷ
   kaṭṭuṇṭu mikavum kacaṅkiṉam eṉṟu kavalaippaṭṭu mukam cāyntiruntatō,
   ellā malaikaḷum pūmiyiṉmēl nilaittirukka nām maṭṭum antarattil nillōm
   eṉat tāḻum nīrmaiyō eṉavellām avaḷ carinta taṉaṅkaḷaip paṟṟic
   camatkāramākak kūṟukiṉṟār.
   ippōtu ciṟṟiṉpa niṉaivillāmal pēriṉpam eytutaṟkē muṟpaṭṭa paṇpiṉāḷ,
   āriyarācaṉaik kāttapirāṉiṉ kaṉivuniṟainta pārvaiyai mēṟkoṇṭāḷ,
   naḷiṉamāṉa irupātattai uṭaiyavaḷ, tēvarkaḷum kāṇa iyalāta
   malaikkoḻuntaik kaṇṭu paṇiyumpaṇi oṉṟē tāṉ pūṇukiṉṟa āparaṇam eṉa
   niṉaikkum peṟṟiyāḷ ākiya ippēriḷampeṇ, nilāveṟikkum muṟṟattē tōḻiyar
   tanta palakaiyai vaittuk kācuparappi, navarattiṉaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu palaṉ
   pārkkum neṟiyaik kaikkoṇṭu, mutalil muttoṉṟaik kaṇṭu itu vēymuttu
   eṉṟāḷ. maṭavār uṭaṉē eṉṉa nī kaṇṭāy eṉavum, aṭuttup pavaḷattaip pārttu
   itaṉiṉum araṉ vēṇi (ceñcaṭai) ciṟantatu ituvum cariyalla eṉakkūṟi
   irattiṉattai nōkki itu koṉṟaiyaṇinta civaṉāriṉ tēkamē eṉṟu ammāmaṇiyait
   taḻuva niṉaitta ellaiyil cūriyaṉaippōla oḷivīcit tēril amarntu
   malaikkoḻuntaṉ aṅku aṇukutalum, pāṅkiyaraip pirintu taṉiyaḷāy vantu
   kumpiṭṭāḷ. aṉpē, poruḷē, aruṭkaṭalē, civamē, muḻumutalē eṉṟu iṟaiñcum
   piṟaraippārāmal, iṉṉamum pārāmukamē paṇṇuvīrō, ārā amutē eṉṟu aṭi
   paṇintāḷ. iṟaivaṉaippārttu kalaikkoḻuntu (piṟaic cantiraṉ) cūṭakkarutum
   nīr eṉ mulaikkoḻuntu cūṭa ēṉ mayaṅkukiṟīr? kāvērinīrāl kaṉivākum nīr,
   eṉ ataranīrai ēṟka ēṉ maṟukkiṟīr? curumpār kuḻaliyiṉ collaikkaṭakka
   virumpāta nīr eṉ vāykkarumpu pōṉṟa, kuḻalicai pōṉṟa collai virumpa ēṉ
   tayaṅkukiṟīr? eṉṉaic caṟṟē tirumpiyākilum pārīrō' eṉṟu tērait toṭarntu
   cellum aḷavil, kakaṉamaṭavārum pūtala miṉṉārum puṭaicūḻa
   māṇikkamalaiyīcaṉ tēr vītiviṭṭu akaṉṟatu eṉa muṭikkiṟār.
   vāṭpōkki ulā - ciṟṟārāycci

   (333) eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
   vāṭpōkki nātaṉai: kaṇṇukkiṉiya vāṭpōkkiyāṉ (304) civāyanakar āti (150)
   malaikkoḻuntar (201) malaikkoḻuntāṉ (305) malaikkoḻuntu (381, 395.)
   māṇikkakkūttar (157) māṇikkakkūttaṉ (64, 65, 329),
   māṇikkamalaikkoḻuntaṉ (149) māṇikkamalai mēviṉōṉ (197) māṇikkamāmaṇi
   (241) muṭittaḻumpu pūṇṭapirāṉ (26) meytanta muṭittaḻumpaṉ (149)
   muttaḻumparāṉa muṭittaḻumpar (361) vēmpai virumpuvār (170) eṉavum
   pukalvār .
   iṟaiviyai: amalai (61) vaṭivāḷ (242) eṉavum vāṭpōkki ampikaiyai,
   curumpār kuḻal, (194, 404) curumpār kuḻali (39, 342) eṉavum
   tirumālai: (aripiramar (388) kamalamalarkkaṇṇaṉ (392), pāṟkaṭalōṉ
   (128), māl (54, 194) valampuri kaikkoṇṭōṉ 109) viṭai (202)
   ayaṉai: ayaṉ (54) aripiramar (388) ticaimukattōṉ (316) paṉ iruvar
   (ayaṉ, māl 393) maṟaiyōṉ (128) vētaṉ (318, 352, 192) vētā (45).
   vināyakarai: vināyakaṉ (129) arivaricēr kārkkuñcaramukaṉ (kāppu)
   murukaṉaic cēy (129) eṉavum kūṟukiṉṟār.
   maṉmataṉai: aṉaṅkaṉ (327) kārmataṉ (204) cittacaṉ (207) mataṉ (278)
   mataṉ ampu añcum (87) mataṉ kēṭakam (284) mataṉ cilaiyiṟ pūṭṭum kaṇai
   (283) mataṉaṉ maṇṭapamcēr katali (293), mataṉum cantittāṉ (272) mataṉ
   cilaiyiṟ pūṭṭum kaṇai (283) mataṉai muṉ pārttakaṇ (312) eṉa
   uraikkiṉṟār.
   vāṭpōkki: nakarai kākam aṇukākkiri (46) civāyanakar (kāppu, 57, 73,
   139) teṉ civatai (236, 408) nimpavaṉam (41) maṇiyāṟ ceykuṉṟu (261)
   māṇikkamalai (58, 97, 149, 197) māṇikkaveṟpu (12,75) ratṉācalam (175)
   vāṭpōkki (13, 139, 165), eṉak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
   iṟaivaṉ tiruvulā vanta tērai: aḻaku maṇittēr (242) āḻi maṇittēr (278)
   ulakaratam aṉaiya cempoṉ maṇittēr (123) kaṉakattēr (307) citramaṇittēr
   (362) nīṭumaṇittēr (397) poṟṟēr (313) poṉmaṇittēr (203) maṇittēr (156,
   237, 245) māmaṇittēr (367) vāmparittēr (335) veṟṟimaṇittēr (199) eṉa
   varṇikkiṉṟār.
   vāṭpōkki nakarat tīrttaṅkaḷai: urōmaca tīrttam (35) kulica naṟṟaṭam
   (33) teyvat tiruccuṉai (38) catākati naṟṟaṭam (33) nāka tīrttam (34)
   katirvīcum cuṭarttaṭam (34) pōtaka tīrttam (35) vintaippoykai (36)
   parattuvācattaṭam, (36) pañcākkaraccuṉai (37) pañcākkaramaṭu (255)
   varākattaṭañcuṉai (39) eṉa icaittuḷḷār.
   vāṭpōkkip purāṇac ceytikaḷai: āriyavaracaṉukku aruḷiya ceyti 21ām
   kaṇṇimutal 26 kaṇṇikaḷilum, āyiraṅkuṭam (22) āriyaṟku mutti koṭuttāṉ
   (334) āriyaṉ muṉ āṉa araṉ (148) āriyaṉaik kāttapirāṉ (378) iṟaivaṉ
   (361) pocivāci (164) pocivācikkaṇakku (23) pocivāci koḷvār (330)
   muṭittaḻumpu (77) muṭittaḻumpu pūṇṭapirāṉ (26) meytanta muṭittaḻumpaṉ
   (149) eṉṟum,
   vāyuvum āticēṭaṉum pūcitta ceytiyai: kaṉṉi, tuṟkai, nākam,
   kaṭavuḷarkōṉ, kāl, iravi, iṭi māmuṉivar (406) eṉṟa kaṇṇiyilum iṭinākam
   (361) kulavaraiyaik kāliṭantē (48), nākēcaṉ (389) nākattiṉ pūcai (306)
   vantikkum mārutattai (31) eṉavum,
   intiraṉ pūcitta ceytiyai: āyiraṅ kōcaṅkaḷum kaṇṇākat tokuttiṭṭōṉ (19,
   406) maṭutta viṉai nītta purukūtaṉ (78) eṉṟa kaṇṇikaḷilum,
   iṭipūcitta ceytiyai: iṭiyēṟu apiṭēkam ceyya varam aruḷiṉōṉ (18, 361
   406) kaṇṇikaḷilum,
   tuṟkaikkaruḷiya ceyalai: ēmavaṭivāḷ (28) kāḷi vaṭukaṉ cāttaṉ (131)
   cantirakācavāḷ (49) tuṟkai (49) piramatti vīṭṭiṉatu (30, 223)
   urōmacamuṉivar pūcitta ceytiyai: oru muṉivaṉ (14) eṉṟa kaṇṇiyilum,
   cūriyaṉ pūcittup pēṟupeṟṟatai, ‘ceṅkatirōṉ tāṉavar ceytīmai tamaiyoḻiya
   (17) eṉavum,
   ‘akattiyarkkaruḷiya ceytiyai kumpayōṉikketirpōy kūṟiya pañcākkarattiṉ
   vimpamāy eṉṟum viḷaṅkuvōṉ (20) eṉavum,
   ‘ciṟṟāyarkkaruḷiya ceytiyai ākameli āyaṉukkāy aṉṟeritta nāḷ mutalāy'
   (46) eṉavum,
   vairapperumāḷukku aruḷiya ceytiyai, ‘cēyiṉaiyē taṅkaikkaḷittāl
   talaiyaḷippēṉ eṉṟavaṉpāl aṅkat tiyākam aruḷiṉōṉ' (43, 44)eṉavum.
   cuntararukku iṟaivaṉ ittalattil poṉṉiṭum pāṟaiyil poṉṉaic corinta
   ceytiyai 'poṉṉiṭu pāṟaikkayalē pōntaruḷi' (118, 364) eṉavum,
   ceṭṭimāriṭam paṉṉiraṇṭām paṅku peṟṟa ceytiyai (118-121) kaṇṇikaḷil
   ‘kūṭṭup patiṉoṉṟāṉa kōnakarattār muṉpōy... paṉṉiruvarākip palaticaiyum
   kīrtti cela maṉṉiyē vāḻum vaṇikar' eṉavum,
   mēlum 'paṉṉiruvar eṉṉuṭaṉē' (393), 'paṉṉiruvar kūṭṭam paṇi ceyya'
   (198), 'paṉṉiruvar īcaṉ' (269), eṉa nūlil pala iṭaṅkaḷil viravivarac
   ceytuḷḷār.
   āyiṉum ittalattaippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷaik kūṟum pōtu, ‘nēriyar kōṉ
   peṇṭirukkup poṉṉāram kāṭṭiṉāṉ' (30) eṉṟum, 'nīlakaṇṭac cōḻaṉ
   paṇiviṭaikkē kati nalkum iyaṟkaiyāṉ' (42) eṉṟum, 'murukēcaṉiṭṭat
   tūṇṭāmaṇi yiṉ cuṭar viḷakkōṉ (32) eṉṟum kuṟippiṭukiṟār. āṉāl, iṅkuk
   kuṟikkappeṟṟa varalāṟu viḷaṅkavillai. mēlum, iṟaivaṉatu maṇittēraic
   cūḻntu varum periyōrkaḷaik kuṟikkumpōtu, 'vāṭpōkkip paṇṭitar' (139)
   'tiyāka vinōtak kuru' (140) 'māṇikkavācakatēcikar' (141) ‘taṭciṇāmūrtti
   paṇṭitar' (141) 'kampaiyaṉum ceṅkōl celuttivara' (143) 'vēlāyuta mukil'
   (144) eṉak kūṟukiṟār. ivarkaḷum vāṭpōkki nakaril ivvulāvāciriyar
   kālattil vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ivarkaḷaip paṟṟiya piṟa
   vivaraṅkaḷ aṟiyak kūṭavillai.
   vāṭpōkki nātarukkuk kāviriyiliruntu apiṭēka tīrttam koṇṭuvarupavarkaḷ
   ‘tirumañcaṉappaṇṭāram' eṉṟaḻaikkap peṟum ceytiyai ‘paṇṭāranāmam
   parittōrum' (144) eṉṟu viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
   innūlil periya purāṇac ceytikaḷai kaṇṇi 185 (tirukkuṟipputtoṇṭar),
   (134) tiruñāṉacampantar (elumpaip peṇṇākkiyatu), (134-A)
   appūtiyaṭikaḷiṉ putalvaṉai nāvukkaracar uyarppittatu, (135)
   mutalaiyuṇṭa pālaṉaic cuntarar aḻait tatu, (180) tiruñāṉacampantariṭam
   tōṟṟuc camaṇarkaḷ maturaiyil kaḻuvēṟiyatu, (137) caṇṭēcar taṉ tantai
   irukālkaḷaiyum taṭintatu, (249) 339) ciṟuttoṇṭar piḷḷaikkaṟiyamutai
   iṟaivaṉukkiṭṭatu, ivai kūṟappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
   tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟpurāṇacceytikaḷ āṅkāṅkē kuṟikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. valai
   vīciya tiruviḷaiyāṭalai alaikkē valai vīcum ammāṉ' (264), eṉṟum
   vaḷaiyal viṟṟa tiruviḷaiyāṭalai 'kūṭalilē mēvi vaḷai pakarvīr (164)
   eṉṟum, pāṇapattirarukku iṟaivaṉ palakaiyiṭṭatai 'kāriruḷil pāṭiya
   pāṇarkkē palakaiyiṭṭār' (363) eṉṟum, kūntalukku iyaṟkai maṇam uṇṭu
   eṉṉum ceytiyai 'teṉṉavarkōṉ ēṟkai maṇam nāṭa eḻunteṉṟal' (215) eṉṟum
   pēcukiṟār.
   kantapurāṇac ceytikaḷ pala innūlil kuṟikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. takkayākattil
   talaiyiḻanta taṉ māmaṉāṉa takkaṉukku āṭṭuttalai aḷittatai 'āṭṭut
   talaipaṭaitta ammāṉ' (263) eṉṟum, kalaimakaḷ takkayākattil
   mūkkiḻantatai (299), cantiraṉ taṉ kalai tēyappeṟṟatai (302), cūriyaṉ
   taṉ paṟkaḷai iḻantatai (302) kuṟippiṭukiṟār.
   ivai tavira, āṅkāṅkē civapurāṇac ceytikaḷaiyum, kāñci purāṇam,
   kaṉṟāppūr purāṇam, uttarakōcamaṅkai purāṇam, tirukkāḷatti purāṇam pōṉṟa
   itara purāṇac ceytikaḷaiyum kūṟukiṉṟār.
   nūlāciriyar ivvulāvil ēḻuparuvamakaḷiriṉ iyalpukaḷaiyum nayampaṭa pala
   putumaikaḷuṭaṉ kūṟiyiruppatai āṅkāṅku cuvaittuṇaralām.

                  innūlil varum purāṇak kataikaḷ cila

   intiraṉ cāpam nīṅkap peṟṟatu
   kautama muṉivariṉ pattiṉiyākiya akalikaiyiṉpāl iḻaitta piḻaikkāka
   intiraṉ ammuṉivarāl capikkap peṟṟāṉ. vāṭpōkki iṟaivaṉaip pirārttittu
   accāpam nīṅkap peṟṟāṉ (kaṇṇi 19.78)
   tirumāl varāka avatāram ceytatu
   iraṇiyākṣaṉ eṉṉum acuraṉ ivvulakattaik kaṭalil āḻttavē, tirumāl varāka
   avatāram ceytu taṉ kompiṉāl ulakattai maṟupaṭiyum mīṭṭuvittār. (ka. 39)
   mālum ayaṉum kāṇā vaṭiviṉaṉ
   civaperumāṉ vicuvarūpam eṭutta poḻutu, ayaṉ (piramaṉ). aṉṉappakṣi
   vaṭivam eṭuttup paṟantu avaṉ muṭiyait tēṭa muṟpaṭṭāṉ, tirumāl varāka
   rūpam eṭuttu avaṉ aṭiyait tēṭa muṉaintāṉ, muṭivil, iruvarum paramaṉiṉ
   muṭiyaiyum aṭiyaiyum kāṇāmal tirumpiṉar. (ka. 54, 115, 194)
   civaṉ yāṉaittōl pōrttatu
   tārukavaṉattu muṉivarkaḷ tām iyaṟṟiya yākattiliruntu tōṉṟiya yāṉaiyaic
   civaperumāṉ mēl ēva, avarum atai aṭakki ataṉ tōlaiyurittu āṭaiyāka
   aṇintu koṇṭār. (ka. 60, 93)
   kapālattil pali ēṟṟatu
   civaṉaip pōlavē taṉakkum aintu talaikaḷ uṇṭeṉat tarukkittup pēciya
   piramaṉ talaiyaip paramacivaṉ koyyavē, avaṉaip pirumakatti tōṣam
   paṟṟikkoṇṭatu. atu akala, maṇṭaiyōṭṭaik kaiyilēnti, civaṉ yācittu
   accāpattaip pōkkik koṇṭāṉ. (ka. 64)
   kaṇṭam kariyatāṉatu
   tēvarkaḷum acurarkaḷum pāṟkaṭalaik kaṭaintaṉar. atiliruntu amutam
   eḻuntatu. atai uṇṭaṉar tēvar. ulakamellām aḻikka eḻuntatu vācukip
   pāmpiṉ viṣam. ataic civaṉ uṇṭāṉ. ataip parācakti taṉ kaiyiṉāl avaṉ
   kaṇṭat tōṭu niṟuttiṉāḷ. civaṉuṭaiya kaṇṭamum kariyatāṉatu. (ka 60, 99)
   civaṉ kaṅkaiyait talaiyil cūṭiyatu
   pakīrataṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ palakālam tavam ceytamaiyāl kaṅkai civaperumāṉ
   caṭai vaḻiyākap pūmikku vantatu. itaṉ nīrāl pakīrataṉiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷāṉa
   cakara puttirarkaḷ aṟupatiṉāyiravaraiyum paricuttamākkic cuvarkkam
   pukacceytāṉ. (ka. 5.9, 64, 91, 161)
   cuvāyam puvamaṉu
   piramatēvaratu meyyiṉ oru pākattiṟ ṟōṉṟiyavar. catarūpai eṉpāḷai
   maṇantu, eḻupattoru caturyukam pūmiyai āṇṭār.
   kajēntiraṉ
   intayāṉai, oru taṭākattil tāmarai malaraik koyyappōy taṉṉaippaṟṟi
   mutalaiyai iḻukkavum, mutalai itaṉai iḻukkavum āyiram āṇṭu pōr
   naṭantatu. yāṉai taṉ palam ōyntu tutikkaiyai nīṭṭi 'ātimūlamē’ eṉṟu
   ōlamiṭṭu niṟkaiyil itaṉ aḻaippaikkēṭṭa tirumāl itu irukkum
   kuḷakkaraiyai aṭaintu taṉ cakkarattai vīci mutalaiyaik koṉṟu yāṉaiyai
   viṭuvittār.
   tirukkuṟipputtoṇṭar
   ivar oru civaṉaṭiyār. kāñcimānakarattil tuṇiveḷukkum peruvaṇṇār
   kulattaic cērntavar. ivaruṭaiya uṇmaittoṇṭiṉai aṟiya civaperumāṉ orunāḷ
   oru kantaiyuṭuttu ivar muṉ tōṉṟiṉār. tirukkuṟipputtoṇṭar civaṉār
   uṭuttuḷḷa kantaiyiṉ aḻukkiṉaip pōkkit tarukiṟēṉ; tantaruḷvīr eṉṟār.
   taṉakku inta orē kantai uḷatākavum, aṉṟu pakalavaṉ maṟaivataṟkuḷ atait
   tuvaittu ularttit taruvatāṉāl tarukiṟēṉ eṉṟalum appaṭiyē kālantāḻttāmal
   āṭaiyait tōyttu antip poḻutiṟkuḷ taruvatāka icaintu atai vāṅkip pōṉār.
   toṇṭaṉār āṭaiyai tōyttu ularttuvataṟkumuṉ perumaḻai peyya ivar atai
   ulartti, civaṉaṭiyārukkuk koṭukka iyalāmal pōyiṟṟu. civaṉaṭiyār kuḷirāl
   vāṭuvarē eṉṟu varunti tāṉ ceyta kuṟṟattukkākat taṉ talaiyait tuṇi
   tōykkum kaṟpāṟaiyil muṭṭimōtik koṇṭār. accamayam akkaṟpāṟaiyiṉ
   pakkattiliruntu aḻakiya vaḷaittaḻumpupaṭṭa ēkāmparanātaratu
   malarcceṅkaiyāṉatu vantu eḻuntu, muṭṭikkoṇṭa toṇṭaṉāriṉ talaiyaip
   piṭittu niṟuttiyatu. civaperumāṉ umātēviyāruṭaṉ avar muṉtōṉṟik kāṭci
   tantaruḷiṉār.
   elumpaip peṇṇuruvākkiyatu
   tirumayilāppūril civanēcaccelvar eṉṉum peruvaṇikar pūmpāvai eṉṉum taṉ
   peṇṇai tiruñāṉacampantarukkē maṇamuṭittuk koṭuppatu eṉṉum uṟuti
   koṇṭiruntār. orunāḷ pūmpāvai tōḻiyarkaḷōṭu pūñcōlaiyil viḷaiyāṭukaiyil
   pāmpu kaṭittu iṟantār. civanēcar evvaḷavu muyaṉṟum avaḷai uyiruṭaṉ
   eḻuppamuṭiyāmaṟ pōkavē, avaḷ uṭalait takaṉamceytu elumpai oru
   kuṭattiliṭṭuc cēmittu vaittiruntār. ñāṉacampantar tiruvoṟṟiyūriṉiṉṟum
   tirumayilāppūrukku eḻuntaruḷi, civanēcariṉ vēṇṭukōḷukkiṇaṅka, ellōr
   muṉṉilaiyilum 'maṭṭiṭṭa puṉṉai' eṉṟa patikampāṭi, elumpuruvāy irunta
   peṇṇai maṟupaṭiyum pāvaiyār vaṭivu peṟac ceytār.
   appūtiyaṭikaḷ putalvaṉ viṭam nīṅkiyatu
   tirunāvukkaracaraik kāṇāmalē avariṭattup peruṅkātal koṇṭa appūti
   aṭikaḷār tirunāvukkaracarukkut tiruvamutu paṭaitkat tam makaṉāṉa mūtta
   tirunāvukkaracarai tōṭṭattilirukkum vaḷamāṉa vāḻaikkuruttiṉai arintu
   varumpaṭi aṉuppiṉār. avaṉum vāḻaikkuruttai ariyumpōtu oru pāmpu avaṉait
   tīṇṭa, viṣam talaikkēṟi, kīḻē viḻuntiṟantāṉ. tirunāvukkaracar aṅku
   nikaḻnta uṇmai iṉṉateṉa aṟintukoṇṭu, karuṇaikūrntu ‘oṉṟu kolām' eṉṟu
   toṭaṅkum tiruppatikattaip pāṭiyaruḷa, talaikkēṟiya viṣam nīṅkavē
   apputalvaṉ uyirpeṟṟeḻuntāṉ.
   mutalaiyuṇṭa pālaṉai aḻaittatu
   cuntaramūrtti nāyaṉār cēranāṭṭai nōkkit talayāttirai ceytu koṇṭē
   avināciyai vantaṭaintār. aṅku cila āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ aintu vayatuṭaiya
   oru ciṟuvaṉai avvūr maṭuvilirunta mutalaiyoṉṟu iḻuttucceṉṟu
   viḻuṅkiviṭṭatu. putalvaṉai iḻanta peṟṟōr cuntararaiyaṇuki
   vaṇaṅkukaiyil, 'iṉpa makaṉai iḻantavarkaḷ nīṅkaḷō' eṉṟu viṉava avarkaḷ
   peruntuyarōṭu tōṉṟavē, cuntarar ammaṭuvaiyaṇuki 'eṟṟāṉ maṟakkēṉ' eṉṟu
   toṭaṅkum patikam pāṭavē, peruvāy mutalaiyoṉṟu appiḷḷaiyaik karaiyil
   koṇarntu cērttatu.
   caṇṭēcar tantaitāḷ eṟintatu
   vicāracarumar eṉṉum caṇṭēcar āṉiraikaḷai mēyttu varuṅkāl,
   maṇṇiyāṟṟaṅkaraiyiluḷḷa āttimarattiṉ kīḻē, civaperumāṉ uruvamākiya
   liṅkattai maṇalāl amaittu ataṟkup pālapiṣēkam ceytu makiḻntu vantār.
   ivar ippaṭic ceyvataikkaṇṭa ayalāṉ oruvaṉ paḻikūṟi, caṇṭēcaruṭaiya
   tantaiyiṭattu itaikkūṟa avarum orunāḷ āṉiraikaḷiṉ piṉceṉṟu kurāmaram
   oṉṟiṉmēl ēṟi, taṉ makaṉ ceyalaik kaṇṇārak kaṇṭār; aṅkuc ceṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa
   pāṟkuṭaṅkaḷaik kāliṉāl iṭaṟic cintiṉār. caṇṭēcar iṟaivaṉukkeṉṟu
   vaittirunta pālaik kālāl utaitta taṉ tantaiyiṉ irukālkaḷaiyum aṅkirunta
   oru kōlaik koṇṭu tuṇittu vīḻttiṉār. tantaiyum maṇṇil vīḻntiṟantār.
   civaperumāṉ aṅkuttōṉṟi caṇṭēcar tantaikkuk kayilāyap pataviyai
   aḷittaruḷiṉār.
   APPENDIX
   MADRAS DISTRICT GAZETTEERS (1907), TRICHINOPOLY
   By F. R. HEMINGWAY - INDIAN CIVIL SERVICE
   (Page 284)
   Ratnagiri: Five miles south-south-west of Kulittalai. Is a hamlet of
   Sivayam, (population 3,338) and takes its name from the curious bare
   conical rock (the Ruby Hill) which there rises suddenly from the
   surrounding plain. This is 1,178 feet high and is ascended by a flight
   of 952 steps which are said (in an inscription in the temple on the
   top) to have been built in 1783 A.D.
   The temple on the top is a handsome, though small building, and an
   admirable view is obtained from it. On the walls are numerous
   inscriptions, two of which copied by the Government Epigraphist (Nos.
   102-03 of 1892) belong to the Hoysala king Vira Somesvara who occupied
   much of the district in the 13th century.
   The god is known as Ratnagirisvarar (the lord of the Ruby Hill) or,
   more popularily, Sokkar. Like the deity of Kadambarkoil near Kūlittalai
   (q.v.) the god is said to have been worshipped by Agastya, and his
   sacred days are Mondays in Kartigai and the Tai-pusam day. Thunder is
   said to come and worship the god once every twelve years, and a crevice
   in the roof is pointed out as the place through which the thunder made
   its entry in order to do so.
   The water for the god's bath is brought by a caste of non Brahmins
   known as Tirumanjana Pandarams, who fetch it every day from the
   Cauvery. They say they are descended from the Aryan king who came to
   the god with the hope of getting rubies from him. The god, in the guise
   of a brahmin, tested his devotion by making him fill a magic vessel
   with Cauvery water. The vessel would not fill, and the Aryan stranger
   in a fit of anger cut off the Brahmin's head. The dead body at once
   turned into a lingam and the Aryan was ordered to carry water for the
   temple till eternity. A figure of him is carved in the prakaram
   (arcade) of the temple.
   There are two shrines subordinate to the Ratnagiri god, namely that to
   Vaira Perumal at the foot of the hill and the small belly god temple
   half way up the hill. Vaira Perumal is said to have been a man of the
   weaving caste from Conjeevaram who cut off his own head here in
   fulfilment of a vow and was deified accordingly. His image has no head.
   He is worshipped with animal sacrifices, and oaths sworn before him are
   held to be particularly binding. The belly-god is called 'the watcher
   of the crows'. It is said that he cursed the crows for upsetting a
   devotee's milk-pot, and that they dare not now come near the temple.
   The bites of snakes and venomous reptiles are said to be innocuous on
   this hill.
   The Ratnagiri temple is held specially sacred by two small communities,
   namely, the Malai-kanda Vellalans (the Vellalans who watch the hill),
   who never leave the neighbourhood and must gaze at the hill every
   morning when they get up; and the twelfth Chettis referred to in the
   account of Mahadanapuram above. It was the god of Ratnagiri who took
   the twelfth share of their property.
   MADRAS DISTRICT GAZETTEERS
   STATISTICAL APPENDIX FOR TRICHINOPOLY DISTRICT
   VOL. II, 1931
   Page 285: Add at the end of the page the following: The trustees of the
   temple are members of the “Twelfth Chetti” caste, whose office is at
   the flight of steps leading to the shrine on the hill. The community
   contributes a twelfth of the income of its members to this temple, and
   this is devoted to daily worship and to various acts of charity at
   festival time.

   APPENDIX II
   MADRAS DISTRICT GAZETTEERS - TRICHINOPOLY
   by
   F. R. HEMINGWAY - INDIAN CIVIL SERVICE
   (Page 281)
   Mahadanapuram: Eight miles west of Kulittalai. Population 6,545.
   Contains a railway - station, a private choultry (where all castes are
   given accommodation, but Brahmans alone are fed) and a saltpetre
   refinery. The village is said to have been given by Krishna Udaiyar of
   Mysore to some Brahmans. Hence its name, which means village of the
   great gift. It possesses a ham let called Palayasengadam. This name is
   supposed to be a contraction of Palaya-Jayanconda-Cholapuram in the
   Udayarpalayam taluk. The latter name means 'the town of the victorious
   Chola, and Palayasengadam is locally believed to have been once a Chola
   capital. It contains some Jain remains, the ruins of an expensive camp
   and a beautifully constructed stone tank, which lend some support to
   this theory of departed greatness.
   In this hamlet is a well-known and blood-thirsty village goddess called
   Alahanachi Amman, in whose honour a festival is celebrated every two
   years in Panguni (March - April). The goddess conveys her assent to the
   performance of the festival by the chirping of the lizards round her
   shrine.
   Palayasengadam is the head-quarters of a section of the Chettis called
   the Pannirendam (twelfth) Chettis. These are supposed to be descended
   from eleven youths who escaped long ago from Kaveripatnam, a ruined
   city in Tanjore. A Chola king, says the legend, wanted to marry a
   chetty girl, whereupon the caste set fire to the town and only these
   eleven boys escaped. They rested on the Ratnagiri hill to divide their
   property; but however they arranged it, it always divided itself into
   twelve shares instead of eleven. The god of Ratnagiri then appeared and
   asked them to give him one share in exchange for a part of his car.
   They did so; and they now call themselves the 'twelfth’ Chettis, from
   the number of the shares, and at their marriages they carry the
   bridegroom round in a car. They are said to be common in Coimbatore
   district.

   mēṟṟicaikkup pōtakamum mēvuvaṭa mēṟkumaṟai
   cāṟṟum urōmacaraṉ cārtaṭamum - cīṟṟam         35
   porutavaṭa pālvintaip poykaiyum īcāṉam
   taruparattu vācat taṭamum – perukiyē        36
   aṭṭatikkum mēvaaṇukiṉōr pāvamelām
   kaṭṭaṟukkum pañcāk karaccuṉaiyum – toṭṭavuṭaṉ        37
   valviṉaiyai nīkki maṟuceṉmam ākkiyaruṭ
   celvanalkum teyvat tiruccuṉaiyum - tollaināḷ 38
   ōṅkukaṭal āḻnta ulakaiyoru kōṭṭataṉāl
   tāṅkum varākat taṭañcuṉaiyum - pāṅkuṟavē         39
   cūḻntutaṉai vāḻttum curumpār kuḻaliyiṭam
   vāḻntu piriyā makimaiyāṉ - tāḻntavarkkē         40
   tāṉiḻalāy niṉṟār tamakkum oruniḻalāy
   māniḻalcey nimpa vaṉattiṉāṉ – pāṉukulam         41
   tōṉṟutiru nīlakaṇṭac cōḻaṉ paṇiviṭaikkē
   ēṉṟukati nalkum iyaṟkaiyāṉ – mūṉṟulakum         42
   mēyatavat tōreṇmar vēṇṭiyē pūcikkat
   tūyavaram īyntaruḷum tōṉṟalāṉ - cēyiṉaiyē         43
   taṅkaik kaḷittāl talaiyaḷippēṉ eṉṟavaṉpāl
   aṅkat tiyākam aruḷiṉōṉ - paṅkayattu         44
   vētā amaippum vilakumeṉac cārntavarkku
   mātāṉam muṟṟum vaḻaṅkuvōṉ – tātārum         45
   tacāṅkam
   kiri
   ākameli āyaṉukkāy aṉṟeritta nāḷmutalāyk
   kākam aṇukāk kaṉakiriyāṉ - māka niṟai         46
   āṟu
   kaṟpakappūṅ kāveṉavē kāmit tatukoṭukkum
   aṟputamāṅ kāvēri yāṟṟiṉāṉ - poṟpamarum         47
   nāṭu
   kōṭṭiṉāl ōṅku kulavaraiyaik kāliṭantē
   nāṭṭiyiṭum kuṉṟucūḻ nāṭṭiṉāṉ – vāṭṭiṉār         48
   nakar
   kaṟkaraiya vīcucantra kācavāḷ peṟṟataṉāl
   tuṟkaitāṉ māṟit toṭarntetirē – niṟkum         49
   varaman timamā mayiṭā curaṉ meyp
   piramat tiyāṉai piḷiṟa - uramuṟṟu         50
   maṇṇañca vīṭṭunāḷ vāṉōr civāyaveṉa
   naṇṇum civāya nakariṉāṉ – viṇṇavarkkuḷ        51
   kutirai
   vāciyāṉ evvuyirkkum maṉṉumiṭai piṅkalaiyūr
   vāciyāṉ eṉṟuraikkum vāciyāṉ – pācanilai        52
   yāṉai
   cētikkum nāṟkaraṇat tiṉmuṭicēr meyññāṉa
   ātik kaṭakkamatat (tu) āṉaiyāṉ cōtikkum        53
   mālai
   mālayaṉum kāṇā vaṭivil puṉaintaruḷum
   mālaiyiṉum poṉṉitaḻi mālaiyāṉ - kālateṉa         54
           muracu
   mūlat tiṭaikiḷaittu mummaṇ ṭalaṅkaṭantuḷ
   ālikkum nātat (tu) aṇimuracāṉ - ñālattaic         55
   koṭi
   cuṭṭaḻala tākkum cuṭalaiyiṉum taṟpiriyāk
   koṭṭamikum ēṟṟuk koṭiyiṉāṉ – maṭṭalāp        56
   āṇai
   piṇṭamum nātāntap peruveḷiyum palkōṭi
   aṇṭamumām añceḻuttiṉ āṇaiyāṉ-oṇṭoṭiyār 57
   irattiṉakiriyīcaṉiṉ perumpukaḻ
   ārkkumā ṇikkamalai ākattāṉ - pōṟṟumicai
   yārkkumā ṇikkamalai yākattāṉ - pārkkuḷ         58
   matikkavaru māṟu malaiyāṉ caṭaiyil
   matikkavarum āṟu malaiyāṉ - tutikkavarum         59
   kaṇṭaṅ kariya kariyuṭaiyāṉ nīlamaṇik
   kaṇṭam kariya kariyuṭaiyāṉ – toṇṭiṉ         60
   amalaiyoru pākat (tu) aṇaivāṉ piṟavā
   tamalai yorupākat (tu) aṇaivāṉ – camaramuka         61
   vīraṉmārt tāṇṭaṉ vitimutalōr pōṟṟutiti
   vāraṉ civayōka mākaraṇaṉ - ōrumaṉak         62
   kēttiraṉpañ cāṅkaṉ kirīcaṉ ama rēcaṉeṉṟu
   tōttirañcey vētat tuḻaṉiyāṉ - pāttiraṅkam         63
   pāṇiyāṉ vēṇiyāṉ pāṇic caṭaimuṭiyāṉ
   kāṇiyāṉ vāṇiyāṉ kāṇiyāṉ - māṇikkak         64
   kūttaṉmā cittiraiyāṉ kūṟiṉavai kāciyāṉ
   āttaṉ namaṉukkē āṉiyāṉ – ēttucapai         65
   āṭiyāṉ āvaṇiyāṉ āṉa puraṭ (ṭu) āciyāṉ
   mūṭimāl aippaciyāṉ muttamiḻkkē - nāṭivaruṅ         66
   kārttikaiyāṉ koṉṟaiyiṉuṅ kāmikkum mārkaḻiyāṉ
   vārttai mikutaiyāṉ māciyāṉ - mūrttikamām         67
   vimpaṅ kuṉiyāṉ virumpupala mātattāṉ
   nampu varuṭa nakumukattāṉ - pampu                 68
   corūpani tāṉa cutāṉa makēca
   virūpaka ṇēca vinōta - arūpa                 69
   nirampiya kaṇṭa niṟaintu paranta
   araṉparaṉ ampalavaṉ aṉṟu - tiruntalarai         70
   vāṭṭuñ cuvā yampu maṉuccit tiraimātam
   ōṭṭu tiru nāḷil ukantorunāḷ – nāṭṭiyapū        71
   pavaṉi
   kāppaṇitalum koṭiyēṟṟamum
   maṇṭalamē luṟṟiravi maṇṭalampuk kāmeṉavē
   viṇṭalantōy maṇṭapattil vīṟṟiruntē - toṇṭarukku 72
   meykkāp paruḷa virumpucivā yannakaril
   kaikkāp paṇintu karuṇaiyuṭaṉ - mikka         73
   koṭiyēṟṟi vīti kulaviyapiṉ antap
   paṭiyē yeḻuntaruḷip pāṉu - neṭitāṉa         74
   kēcāti pātamāka aṇikaḷ aṇinta vakai
   māṇikka veṟpai valañceytu poṉmalaiyaip
   pūṇikkai yāyttoḻavē pōṉataṟpiṉ - kāṇikkai 75
   vāṅkumaṇi nīlam malarkkaratti lēyiruntu
   vīṅkucuṭar mēluṟṟa vimpameṉa - ōṅku         76
   muṭittaḻumpiṉ cōrituḷḷi muṉṉuṟainta teṉṉa
   aṭut (tu) etirē niṉṟaṅ kaḻakāy - maṭuttaviṉai         77
   nīttapuru kūtaṉ niḻaleṉavē vāṉavaraik
   kāttamaṟu mēlpōyk kacintateṉap - pārt (tu) ericey 78
   kākamē liṉṉaṅ kaṉaṟkaṇ pukaintateṉap
   pākaparañ cevvi paṭintateṉa – mēkamuṭaṉ         79
   teḷḷu tiruccāntu tilakamaṇin tēkoṭiya
   puḷḷulavā muṉṟiliṭai pōyttirumpi - veḷḷiyeṉa 80
   miṉṉum pacuṅkiraṇa veṇpaṭṭu mēṟcāttit
   tuṉṉuṅ kaṭukkait toṭaicātti - maṉṉuṅ         81
   kalavaik kuḷircāttik kaṟpūrañ cāttic
   calavaic ceḻuñcāntu cātti - ulakutoḻu         82
   māmutuvar veṇcā maraiyiraṭṭa vāṇu talār
   tāmum palakītan tāṉpāṭak - kāmarupūn         83
   teṉṟaṟ koḻuntulavac centīpa rāciyuṭaṉ
   niṉṟucci viṇṇiṉ nilaveṟippa - muṉṟiliṭaip         84
   puṅkavarkaḷ cūḻntu pukaḻntuvarap pōntaḻakāy
   aṅkaṇ iṉituṟ (ṟu) aruḷiyapiṉ - poṅkiyeḻum         85
   avviyantīr caivattu arumaṟaiyōr ākamattiṉ
   tivviyamām pūcai celuttutalum - evvamataṉ         86
   ampañcum añcā (tu) aṭalpuriya añcuvarpōl
   cempoṉ cey paḷḷiyaṟai cērntaṇuki – vampañcum 87
   ammai mulai pulli akilāṇ ṭamumpurakkuñ
   cemmai yoṭuṅkaḷapac cēṟāṭi - mummaiyuntāṉ         88
   cīlit taṟiyuñ civayōkam uṭkiḷara
   ālit tuṟaṅkum aṉantaliṉaik - kālattē         89
   vanteḻuppu māyiravi vāṉavaṉkai nīṭṭutaluṅ
   kantamala rōtiyuṭaṉ kaṇmalarntē - antarattuk         90
   kaṅkainī rātik kaṭavuḷarpōyk koṇṭuvaruñ
   ceṅkai nīr malkun tiruccaṭaimēl - muṅkiṉōr         91
   tukka mayalakaṟṟum tūyapoṉṉi nīrāṭi
   mikka nava pūcai vitikoṇṭu - tikkuṭaitāṉ         92
   kaṭṭuṇṭu māviṉ kalaikariya (tu) āyppoṟimēṟ
   paṭṭa puliyāṭai paḻuteṉṟē - viṭṭeṟintu         93
   cempoṟ kalaiyuṭuttē tēcu peṟumakilaṅ
   kampit tiṭātamarum kaccacaittē - umparukkuḷ         94
   ōṅkuporuḷ mūṉṟum oruporuḷāy niṉṟateṉa
   vāṅkum upavītam mārpaṇintu - pāṅkuṭaṉē         95
   kēcātipātam
   ellā niṟaiporuḷum eyti muṭiviṭattē
   villār maṇimakuṭam vēyntaruḷik - kallāram         96
   mikkamā ṇikkamalai vēḻattiṉ ōṭaiyeṉat
   takkanutaṟ paṭṭam tarittaruḷi - ekkiyapaṇ         97
   pāṭum iruvar payiliṭattē poṟkuḻaiyum
   tōṭum aṇintu tulaṅkavē - ōṭiyataṉ         98
   maṅkai viṭattāl maṇikkan tarampiṭitta
   ceṅkaiyiṟpoṉ mālai ciṟakkavē – aṅkaṇuyar        99
   vēymuttuk kaṇṭacaram vēṇmārpi lēyilaka
   vāymutta miṭṭu vacīkarikka - cēymuttu         100
   kantaranī lattaiyoḷi kāṇap puṟamotuṅki
   vantateṉak kēyūram vāḷvitirppa - mantaramāyt         101
   tokkamaṇi oṉpatiṉāṟ cūḻntucuṭar kālturuvac
   cakkaranēr tōḷ valaiyan tāṉpiṟaṅka - mikka         102
   urukkoṇṭu palkatirum oṉṟāṉa teṉṉat
   tarukku maṇippatakkan tāḻa - nerukkiyaṇi         103
   poṟpāntaḷ īṉṟa putiyamaṇi mālikaiyum
   veṟpān takaiya veyileṟippak - kaṟpānta         104
   veḷḷan taṉaiyuṟiñca vempacikoṇ ṭēyirunta
   kaḷḷamaḻu vōrceṅ karamaruvat - tuḷḷucaṭait         105
   tivviyamām paccaic ceḻumpuṟ koḻuntataṉaik
   kavvuvapōl ōrkaik kalaikutikka - evvuyirkkum         106
   puṇṭarikat tāḷē pukaliṭamām eṉpataṉaik
   kaṇṭaṟiyum īṅkeṉa vōr kainīṭṭa - eṇṭicaikkum 107
   kāṭci taruñcamaiyaṅ kāṇituveṉ ṟēyuṇarttuñ
   cūṭciyoṭu ceṅkait tuṭituṭikka - māṭci         108
   valampurikaik koṇṭōṉ malarkkaṇuṟu tāḷmēṟ
   cilampalampi mōtic cilampa - alampu         109
   talaimālai yāṭac caṭaiyum piṟaiyum
   alaimōti yāṭa aṇiyum - malaritaḻi         110
   mālai tuvaṇṭāṭa māmaṟaikoṇ ṭāṭavaṉpar
   vēlai parantu miṭaintāṭap - pālaṉaiya         111
   caṅkam atirat taṭāri muḻavatirat
   tuṅka muracat tokaiyatira - eṅkaḷammai         112
   kāvik kiraṇamalark kaṇṇukantu kākkavē
   tāvit tiruvāṭu taṇṭēṟi - mūvulakum         113
   uṟṟamataṉ āṭaṟ kutaviceya vēnilavum
   koṟṟa mativeṇ kuṭainiḻaṟṟac - cuṟṟiyeḻun(tu)         114
   iṉṉamunā ṭaṉṉam etirvantu kāṇpateṉa
   vaṉṉamali veṇcā maraiyacaiyat - taṉṉaiyē         115
   mōcamaṟak kaṇṭōr muṭiyā viṉaiyakala
   vīcuvapōl ālavaṭṭam vīcavē - tēculavum         116
   vimpamaṇik kāḷāñci mikka aṭaippaivarac
   cempoṟ pirampu cilarēnta - amparamum         117
   poṉṉiṭupāṟaikkayalē vantu maṇit tēr ēṟutal
   maṉṉumaṇik kōpurattiṉ vācal palakaṭantu
   poṉṉiṭupā ṟaikkayalē pōntaruḷi - muṉṉorunāḷ 118
   kūṭṭupati ṉoṉṟāṉa kōnakarat tārmuṉ pōy
   nāṭṭumiti ṉāmōr nakarameṉat - tēṭṭamatāy         119
   īcaṉār colla icaivukoṇṭa nāḷmutalāy
   mācilātu ōṅku makimaiyuṭaṉ - tēcamēṟ         120
   paṉṉiruva rākip pala ticaiyuṅ kīrtticela
   maṉṉiyē vāḻum vaṇikaraṉpāl - piṉ iyaṟṟum         121
   ātavaṉum cantiraṉum āḻi vaṭivamatām
   mātam ayaṉam varuṭamutal – ōtupala         122
   umparkaḷum meyyām ulaka ratamaṉaiya
   cempoṉmaṇit tērēṟit tēviyuṭaṉ - pampai         123
   vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ muḻakkam
   taṭāri tuṭitimilai taṇṇumai kaittāḷam
   viṭāta muracumaṇi mēḷaṅ - kuṭamuḻā         124
   caṅku timiri tavilpaṭa nākacura
   maṅkala kītamutal vāttiyamum - poṅkiyeḻa         125
   piccam mutaliya ciṉṉam cūḻntu varal
   māmaṇikkāṟ picca vaṇikaḷ piṟaṅkiyiṭat
   tūmaṇiveḷ ḷēṟṟut tuvacamuṟac - cōmaṉeṉum         126
   veṇcan tiravaṭṭam mēlacaintu tāḻvateṉat
   taṇcan tiravaṭṭai tāṉcuḻaṟṟap - paṇcaruvip         127
   piramaṉ mutaliyōr tattam vākaṉaṅkaḷil varutal
   pāṭumaṟai yōṉaṉamum pāṟkaṭalōṉ puḷḷēṟum
   nāṭumuṉai vīraṉ navilviṭaiyum – nīṭoḷiyāy        128
   viṇṭavaḻu mikkōr vimāṉamaṇit tērmicaiyiṉ
   aṇṭavi nāyakaṉ cēy ākumayil - eṇṭicaiyōr         129
   māmaṟika ṭāppūta macca miralainaku
   tēmaruvu puṭparatañ cēviṉilun - tāmuṟavē         130
   kāḷi vaṭukaṉ kaṉalaṉaiya cāttaṉivar
   āḷi ñamali yaṭukaḷiṟum - vāḷiyaṅkaṭ         131
   cattakaṉṉi māreṇmar cāṟṟum patiṉoruvar
   nittiya māmiruvar nītiyoṭun - tattam         132
   vimāṉa micaiyēṟa viñcaiyar kantarppar
   umāpatiyeṉ ṟētti uvappak - kumāriyeṉa         133
   attiyai yumpāvai yākkiṉār paṇpāṭi
   muttuc civikaiyiṉ mēl muṉṉaṭakka - etticaiyuṅ         134
   [pōṟṟumap pūti putalvaṉ arāviṭamē
   pāṟṟumavar tēvāram pāṭivarac - cāṟṟum]
   karānuṅkuñ cēyiṉaiyuṅ kāmit taḻaittār
   irācaciṉṉa mōṭuṟ ṟiṟaiñcat - tarāpatimuṉ         135
   vāmpariya tāka vaṉanariyaik kāṇpittār
   kāmpiṉara vākaṉat(tu) aṇuka – ēmpaluṭaṉ         136
   tantaiyiru tāḷtaṭinta caṇṭēcaṉ kēṭakattil
   vantaruḷa aṉpar vaṇaṅkavē – entai         137
   āticaivak kurumār mutaliyōr ēttic cūḻntuvaral
   paravaimaṉaik kēkap paravutamiḻ vēntaṉ
   marapataṉi lutittu vantē - kuruvaṭivu         138
   koṇṭu civāyanakark kōyi liṭattuṟaiyum
   paṇṭitarām vāṭpōkkip paṇṭitarum - eṇṭicaimēṟ 139
   cellum pukaḻcēr tiyākavinō takkuruvuñ
   collum paḻaiya curutiyuṇar - vallamaiyāṉ         140
   maṇṭalañcēr māṇikka vācakanaṟ ṟēcikarum
   muṇṭakaṉēr taṭcaṇā mūrttiyeṉum - paṇṭitaruñ         141
   caiva civākamattut tānikarām vētiyarum
   teyva maṟaiyōrum tiraṇṭētta – vaiyamelām        142
   pērāṉa kīrttip periyōrpāṟ kampaiyaṉum
   cīrāṉa ceṅkōl celuttivarap - pārōrum         143
   viṇṭārum mātavarum vēlā yutamukilum
   paṇṭāra nāmam parittōrum - koṇṭāṭik         144
   kottaṭimai yāṉōruṅ kōyiṟ kaṇakkaruṭaṉ
   uttamamāṅ kūla muṭaiyārum - nittiyamum         145
   kalluṅ karaiyak kaṉintavicai pāṭivarum
   mullai nakaiyār mutalaṭimai - ellavarum         146
   tirucciṉṉaṅkaḷ kaṭṭiyaṅkūṟal
   cūḻntu varavē curutimuṭik keṭṭāmal
   vāḻntacivaṉ vantāṉ vaḻivilakit - tāḻntakuṟai         147
   tīravē nāḷun tiṉakarcakā yaṉvantāṉ
   āriyaṉmuṉ ṉāṉa araṉvantāṉ - pār aṟiya         148
   vantamā ṇikka malaikkoḻuntaṉ vantāṉmey
   tanta muṭittaḻumpaṉ ṟāṉvantāṉ - entametir         149
   ceṉṟapirāṉ vantāṉ civāyanaka rātivantāṉ
   koṉṟaiyaṇi pārvaik kuḷaṉvantāṉ - eṉṟu tiruc 150
   makaḷir kuḻāṅkaḷ
   ciṉṉaṅkaḷ ārppat tikantamaṭa vāruṭaṉē
   maṉṉuṅ kakaṉa maṭantaiyaruṅ - kiṉṉarartam         151
   kaṉṉiyarum nākariḷaṅ kaṉṉiyarum viñcaiyarkaḷ
   kaṉṉiyarum cittariḷaṅ kaṉṉiyarum - miṉṉaṉaiya         152
   mānilamiṉ ṉārtam vaṭivameṭut tēkaṉaka
   mēṉilai māṭattu mēṭaiyiṉum – vāṉaḷavu         153
   maṇṭapattum cāḷarattum māḷikaiyum cūḷikaiyum
   viṇṭaṭavu kōpurattum meyttatēp - puṇṭarika         154
   valli yaṉaiya maṭavār makamēru
   villi yetirēpōy virakamatāy - nillumeṉa         155
   ōṭumaṇit tērmēl umāpatiyaip pārt(tu) evaruñ
   cūṭumalark kaiyāṟ ṟoḻutiṭuvār - tēṭariya         156
   māṇikkak kūttarē mārā vaḷimuttaṅ
   kāṇikkai yōcoṟ kaṭiteṇṇīr - pūṇuṟṟa         157
   paccai vaṭivam pacalai niṟa māmemaip pōl
   iccaiyiṟpūṇ ṭīrō veṉakkēḷīr - kaccaravap         158
   pāmpai yaṇaivīr pakarumalkuṟ pāmpaṇaiyac
   cōmpa lēteṉṟu tutittiṭīr - vīmpiṉ         159
   viṭa nukarvīr kāma viṭamakaṟṟa vēṟōr
   iṭamaṟiyī rōveṉ ṟicaiyīr - caṭaiyiloru         160
   māṉaic cumantīr maṭamā ṉaṉaiyārmuṉ

   kacivāci tīrak karutīr - icaiyum                 162

   meyvaḷaiyak kūṭalilē mēvi vaḷai pakarvīr
   kaivaḷaiyai vāṅkak kaṭavīrō - poyvaḷaiyā         164
   vāṭpōkki yāṉāl mataṉpōr kaḷaiyāmal
   nāṭpōkki vīṇai naviṟṟiṉīr - tīṭpiṉuṭal         165
   veṇṇīṟa tāka veḷuppāṉa teṉmaṭavār
   kaṇṇēṟu paṭṭatō kāṇeṉpār - puṇṇēṟi         166
   eyttāraik kāppī reṉavantum emmiṭattil

   paṇiyā paraṇam pataikka vantār mālāl
   taṇivārō veṉṟu taḷarvār - maṇivaṇṇaṉ         168
   kaṇṇiṭantum neñcaṅ kaṉiyā (tu) iruntavarkkup

   pētai

   miṉṉum maṇikkiraṇam mikka maṇi āṭaraṅkil
   taṉṉainikar āyam taḻuvavē - poṉṉumaṇi         190

   maṟaimuḻakkum pēri maṇimuḻakkum vāṉōr

   kōlum curumpār kuḻalpāl irun (tu) evarum

   vārārō inta malaikkoḻuntar tām eṉalum

   taṉṉāl aṟiyat takum vāyāḷ - muṉṉāṉa         209
   kumpamulai mātar kuṟippaṟintu kūṭuvaḷ pōl

   āṭalār ēvumukil aṉṟu nāṉ māṭamēṟ

   kaṉṉiyiḷam pēṭṭekiṉak kāliṉ naṭaiciṟitu

   empirāṉ nāmam icait tapacuṅ kiḷḷaicēr
